Even keeping my calendar clean, which I usually care about, I just… don’t really start.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling more tired or low on energy lately, even when you’re not doing much?\n\nPatient: I don’t know… not really in a clear way. I haven’t been doing much, so it’s hard to tell. I’ve been kind of flat, I guess… but I haven’t really tracked it like that.\n\nDoctor: Have you felt down or low most of the day, nearly every day, for at least two weeks?\n\nPatient: I’m not sure.
